Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The planetary operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 394. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 927 with 4 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the planetary decomposition. By minimality, the element a^394 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 927-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
